<h2 class="section-header__title">Open Source Supplements to Build a Better Brain.</h2>
<div class="rte-setting"><p>We are tired of all the phony advertising, "proprietary formulas", and the shady practices found in the supplement industry. You’ve seen the reports in the news of companies stuffing supplements with cheap fillers or spiking supplements with illegal ingredients. It’s insane.</p>
<p>That's why we launched Natural Stacks, the <a href="https://naturalstacks.com/pages/open-source">world's first open-source supplement company</a>. Open Source means Ingredient Traceability, Full Label Disclosure, and 3rd Party Lab Testing.</p></div>

<h2 class="section-header__title">Funding Alzheimer’s Research</h2>
</div>
<div class="feature-row">
<div class="feature-row feature-row__txt--right">
<div class="feature-row__item feature-row__text feature-row__text--right feature-row__text--">
<div class="feature-row__item-wrapper">
<div class="rte-setting"><p>We have partnered with the Cure Alzheimer's Fund, a 501(c)(3) non-profit organization where <strong>100% of the funds raised go directly to research.</strong></p>
<p>Since its founding over $50,000,000 has been donated to research initiatives resulting in key breakthroughs including accelerated drug testing and a potential treatment.</p>
<p>Every Natural Stacks product sold helps fund research with the highest probability of preventing, slowing or reversing Alzheimer's disease.</p></div>

<script>
      var ajaxCartConfig = {
        formSelector: '#AddToCartForm',
        cartContainer: '#CartContainer',
        addToCartSelector: '#AddToCart',
        cartCountSelector: '#CartCount',
        cartCostSelector: '#CartCost',
        moneyFormat: "${{amount}}",
        disableAjaxCart : true
      }

      jQuery(function($) {
        ajaxCart.init(ajaxCartConfig);
      });

    jQuery(document.body).on('completeAddItem.ajaxCart', function(evt, cart) {

        var addToCartSelector = $('#AddToCart'),
            addedToCartBtnLabel = 'Thank you!';

        addToCartSelector.children().each(function() {
          if ($.trim($(this).text()) !== '') {
            $(this).text(addedToCartBtnLabel);
          }
        });

      });

      jQuery(document.body).on('afterCartLoad.ajaxCart', function(evt, cart) {


        var addToCartSelector = $('#AddToCart'),
            addToCartBtnLabel = 'Add to Cart';
        // Bind to 'afterCartLoad.ajaxCart' to run any javascript after the cart has loaded in the DOM

        timber.RightDrawer.open();

        setTimeout(function () {
          addToCartSelector.children().each(function() {
            $(this).text(addToCartBtnLabel);
          });
        }, 1000);
      });

      jQuery('#PageContainer::before').on('click', function() {
        timber.RightDrawer.close();
      });


    </script>

<h2>
    To guarantee fast shipping and the best possible service, we're transferring you to our Australian website.
  </h2>
<a class="btn btn--secondary" href="https://www.naturalstacks.com.au/" type="button">

    I'll stay here, I understand my order won't be fulfilled

<script type="text/javascript">
  $(window).load(function () {
    function openGeoPopup() {
      $.magnificPopup.open({
        items: {
          src: '#geolocation-popup'
        },
        type: 'inline',

        fixedContentPos: false,
        fixedBgPos: true,

        overflowY: 'auto',

        closeBtnInside: false,
        preloader: false,

        midClick: true,
        closeOnBgClick: false,
        closeOnContentClick: false,
        removalDelay: 300,
        mainClass: 'my-mfp-slide-bottom'

      });
    }

    $(function() {
        var geo_popup = $.cookie('geo_popup');
        var cookie_enabled = true;

      //Location Redirect
      $.getJSON('//get.geojs.io/v1/ip/geo.json', function (data) {

        var bodyClass = $('body').attr('class')
        var customerLocation = data.country_code

        var IS_PRODUCT = bodyClass.indexOf('product') > 0
        var IS_COLLECTION = bodyClass.indexOf('collection') > 0
        var IS_AUSSIE = customerLocation === 'AU'
        var IS_KIWI = customerLocation === 'NZ'

        var SHOULD_SHOW_POP = IS_PRODUCT || IS_COLLECTION
        var IS_BANNED = IS_AUSSIE || IS_KIWI

        // Show message and redirect AU & NZ visitors
        if (cookie_enabled && geo_popup == 'open') {
          return false;
        } else {
          if (SHOULD_SHOW_POP && IS_BANNED) {
            openGeoPopup();
          }
        }
        if (cookie_enabled) {
          $.cookie('geo_popup', 'open', { expires: 10 });
        }

        $('#dismiss-geopopup').on('click', function (event) {
          $.magnificPopup.close();
        })
      })

    });
  });
</script>
